2-2h6"></path></svg></a></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div></div></div></aside><div class="fern-prose prose max-w-none break-words mb-12 space-y-12"><div class="mb-12 space-y-12"><div style="display:contents"><p>Qdrant is a <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/overview/">vector database<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a> and a <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/overview/vector-search/">semantic search<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a> engine. You can use its REST API to develop a production-ready service to store, search, and manage vectors with an additional payload.</p>
<h2 id="how-does-qdrant-work" data-state="closed" style="cursor:pointer">How does Qdrant work?</h2>
<ol class="mb-3 list-outside list-decimal [&amp;_ol]:!list-[lower-roman]">
<li class="">First, you should create a <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v-1-18-x/api-reference/collections/create-collection">collection</a> to store all your data.</li>
<li class="">Then upsert data <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v-1-18-x/api-reference/points/upsert-points">points</a> and enrich them with a custom <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v-1-18-x/api-reference/points/set-payload">payload</a>.</li>
<li class="">With a full collection, run a <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v-1-18-x/api-reference/search/points">search</a> to find relevant results.</li>
<li class="">Collections can be <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v-1-18-x/api-reference/snapshots/list-snapshots">snapshotted, downloaded and restored</a>.</li>
<li class="">When ready, setup a <a class="fern-mdx-link" href="/v-1-18-x/api-reference/distributed/create-shard-key">distributed system</a> for production.</li>
</ol>
<h3 id="just-getting-started" data-state="closed" style="cursor:pointer">Just getting started?</h3>
<p>Try the <a class="fern-mdx-link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/quick-start/">development quickstart<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-external-link external-link-icon"><path d="M15 3h6v6"></path><path d="M10 14 21 3"></path><path d="M18 13v6a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V8a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h6"></path></svg></a> guide.</p>
